Rennick)** prior to `07/29/2025`.\n\n2. **Collection Switchboard `CSB-1` Main Breaker (`2,000 A`) Undersized for 125% of Combined Continuous Inverter Output Current (`2,075 A`) (`Drawing E-201 Rev B` & `Drawing E-301 Rev B`)**\n   - **Issue:** Each skid (`SK-1` to `SK-4`) has a continuous AC output current of `2 \u00d7 162.4 A + 90.2 A = 415.0 A` (`575 kW` at `800 V`). All four skids combine on the `2,500 A` bus of `CSB-1` and flow through the `CSB-1` main breaker to `T-1`, giving a combined continuous inverter output current of **`4 \u00d7 415.0 A = 1,660.0 A`** (`2,300 kW` at `800 V`). At the required 125% factor, minimum OCPD rating is **`1.25 \u00d7 1,660.0 A = 2,075.0 A`**, but `CSB-1` has a **`2,000 A` main breaker** (an 80%-rated `2,000 A` breaker is limited to `1,600 A` continuous `< 1,660 A`).\n   - **Basis:** **2023 NEC 690.8(A)(1)(a), 690.9(B), 705.28(A), 705.30(B), 215.2(A)(1)(a), 215.3, and 240.6(A)** (next standard breaker size above `2,075 A` is **`2,500 A`**).\n   - **Required Action & Responsible Party:** **Tamsworth Electrical Constructors Inc. (R. Vance)** must either replace the `2,000 A` main breaker in `CSB-1` with a **`2,500 A` main breaker** (800 V, 65 kA, matching the `2,500 A` bus and within the 250% secondary limit of **NEC Table 450.3(A)** for `T-1`'s `1,804 A` secondary current: `2,500 / 1,804 = 138.6%`), OR provide manufacturer listing documentation proving the `CSB-1` switchboard assembly and its `2,000 A` main breaker are listed for **100% continuous operation** under **NEC 690.9(B) Exception / 705.30(B) Exception / 215.3 Exception No. 1**, revise `Drawing E-201 Rev B`, and obtain AHJ approval.\n\n3. **Missing Multiple-Source Busbar Warning Label on `CSB-1` (and `ACP-1` to `ACP-4`) and Missing Power-Source Directory Plaque (`Drawing E-201 Rev B` \u2014 `CSB-1` & `Labels`)**\n   - **Issue:** On `CSB-1` (`2,500 A` bus), `2,000 A (main) + 2,075 A (125% PV) = 4,075 A > 3,000 A (120% of bus)`, so `CSB-1` relies on **NEC 705.12(B)(3)(3)** (`4 \u00d7 600 A + 30 A = 2,430 A \u2264 2,500 A` bus; similarly, each `ACP-1..4` has `2 \u00d7 225 A + 125 A = 575 A` on a `600 A` bus). However, `Drawing E-201 Rev B` (`Labels` row) omits the mandatory **NEC 705.12(B)(3)(3)** multiple-source busbar warning label and omits the **NEC 705.10 / 690.56(B)** permanent directory plaque at `POI`/`PSW-1` and `CSB-1` identifying all power sources and disconnects.\n   - **Basis:** **2023 NEC 705.12(B)(3)(3), 705.10, and 690.56(B)**.\n   - **Required Action & Responsible Party:** **Tamsworth Electrical Constructors Inc. (R. Vance)** must apply the permanent **NEC 705.12(B)(3)(3)** warning labels on `CSB-1` and `ACP-1` to `ACP-4`, install the permanent **NEC 705.10** power-source directory plaques at `PSW-1`/`POI` and `CSB-1`, and update `Drawing E-201 Rev B` (`Labels` entry).\n\n4. **Auxiliary Panelboard `AUX-1` & Tracker Controllers `TC-1 to TC-8`: Outdoor Enameled Steel Strut, 800 V Utilization Branch Circuits, Disconnecting Means, and Feeder Emergence (`Drawings E-201 Rev B` & `E-202 Rev B`)**\n   - **Issue:**\n     - *(a) Outdoor Enameled Strut:* `Drawing E-202 Rev B` (`Mounting` tab) mounts outdoor panelboard `AUX-1` and controllers `TC-1 to TC-8` at the array north edge on `\"1-5/8 in. steel strut, green enamel finish\"`. Ferrous metal support hardware protected from corrosion solely by enamel is prohibited outdoors.\n     - *(b) 800 V Utilization Branch Circuits:* `AUX-1` (`800 V, 3-phase, 3-wire, 100 A main lugs`, fed from a `30 A` breaker in `CSB-1` without a step-down transformer) supplies eight 3-pole `15 A` branch circuits at **`800 V`** (`462 V` to ground) to permanently connected utilization equipment (`TC-1 to TC-8`), exceeding the **600 V between conductors** (and 277 V to ground) branch-circuit limitation for utilization equipment.\n     - *(c) Disconnects & Feeder Riser:* `AUX-1` is a freestanding outdoor MLO panelboard with **eight** branch breakers (exceeding the six-disconnect limit of **NEC 225.33(A)** for a structure supplied by a feeder under **225.31** without a local main disconnect); `AUX-1` (`beside TC-1`) is not in sight from distant block controllers `TC-2 to TC-8` across blocks/rows 2 to 8 and lacks specified lockable-open provisions per **NEC 430.102(A) & 110.25**; and the `AUX-1` feeder in `\"1 in. HDPE duct in the collection trench\"` lacks a specified rigid metal or Schedule 80 PVC riser stub-up where emerging above grade.\n   - **Basis:** **2023 NEC 300.6(A), 210.6(D)(2), 210.6(C), 225.31, 225.33(A), 430.102(A), 110.25, 353.12(1)\u2013(2), and 300.5(D)(1), (4)**.\n   - **Required Action & Responsible Party:** **Tamsworth Electrical Constructors Inc. (R. Vance)** must replace the green-enameled strut with hot-dip galvanized/stainless outdoor strut per **300.6(A)**; install an auxiliary step-down transformer (e.g., 800 V to 480 V or 240/120 V) and appropriately rated panelboard/controller inputs so utilization branch circuits do not exceed 600 V per **210.6(D)(2)**; provide a main disconnect at `AUX-1` per **225.31/225.33(A)** and in-sight or 110.25-lockable disconnects for `TC-1 to TC-8` per **430.102(A)**; install a rigid metal or Schedule 80 PVC riser for the `AUX-1` feeder emergence per **353.12 & 300.5(D)**; and revise `Drawings E-201 Rev B` and `E-202 Rev B`.\n\n5. **Tracker Row Slew-Drive Circuits Wired with Open `Type TC` Tray Cable Along Row Piles Outside a Raceway or Cable Tray (`Drawing E-203 Rev B` \u2014 `Wiring`)**\n   - **Issue:** `Drawing E-203 Rev B` (`Wiring` tab) specifies the 96 slew-drive circuits (`48 V dc, 0.25 kW`, 12 rows/block \u00d7 8 blocks) as `\"3 x 12 AWG Cu Type TC, from each block's controller along the row piles to each row's drive\"`\u2014installed as open cable along the tracker row piles without a raceway or cable tray.\n   - **Basis:** **2023 NEC 336.12(1)\u2013(4) and 336.10(4), (7)** (`Type TC` tray cable shall not be installed outside a raceway or cable tray system or as open cable on brackets/cleats/piles).\n   - **Required Action & Responsible Party:** **Tamsworth Electrical Constructors Inc. (R. Vance)** must enclose the slew-drive circuits in an NEC-compliant raceway or cable tray system (with sunlight-resistant cable) or replace the open `Type TC` runs with an NEC-permitted outdoor/underground wiring method (or AHJ-approved protected `Type TC-ER`), revise `Drawing E-203 Rev B`, and obtain AHJ inspection approval.\n\n6. **Skid Inverter Output Circuits Using `Type USE-2` Conductors in Aboveground Rigid Steel Conduit on the Skid Frame (`Drawing E-301 Rev B` \u2014 `Inverter Circuits`)**\n   - **Issue:** `Drawing E-301 Rev B` (`Inverter Circuits` tab) specifies `\"3 x 1/C 4/0 AWG Cu, 2 kV Type USE-2\"` (`NG-XT-225` to `225 A` breaker) and `\"3 x 1/C 1 AWG Cu, 2 kV Type USE-2\"` (`NG-XT-125` to `125 A` breaker) in `\"Rigid steel conduit on the skid frame\"`\u2014an entirely aboveground installation not emerging from the ground.\n   - **Basis:** **2023 NEC 338.12(B)(2)** (`Type USE` prohibited for aboveground installations except where emerging from ground to terminate in an outdoor enclosure).\n   - **Required Action & Responsible Party:** **Tamsworth Electrical Constructors Inc. (R. Vance)** must either verify/document on `Drawing E-301 Rev B` that the installed conductors are dual-rated and marked **`Type RHW-2`** (or `XHHW-2`), OR replace the single-rated `2 kV Type USE-2` conductors in the skid-frame conduits with an NEC-permitted 2 kV conductor type such as **`2 kV Type RHW-2`** (as used on `C-1 to C-4` and `AUX-1`).\n\n7. **`1,500 V dc` (Over 1,000 V) PV String Home Runs in Trench at `600 mm` (`24 in.`) Depth / Without Underground Raceway or Stub-Up Bushing (`Drawing E-301 Rev B` \u2014 `Pads`)**\n   - **Issue:** `Drawing E-301 Rev B` (`Pads` tab) routes `10 AWG PV wire, 2 kV` string home runs with **`maximum dc voltage 1,500 V`** (over `1,000 V`) from the array into `2 in. PVC-coated rigid steel conduit` pad stub-ups that terminate **`600 mm` (`24 in.`) below grade to the trench**, with no underground raceway in the trench and no stub-up end bushings. While `600 mm` (`24 in.`) is the **Table 300.5** direct-burial cover for `\u2264 1,000 V`, circuits **over 1,000 V** require **30 in. (`750 mm`)** minimum cover for direct-buried cables under **NEC Table 300.50** (or **18 in. [`450 mm`]** in nonmetallic raceway / **6 in. [`150 mm`]** in rigid metal conduit).\n   - **Basis:** **2023 NEC 690.31(A), 690.31(B)(1), 690.31(C)(1), 300.50(A) & (C), and Table 300.50**.\n   - **Required Action & Responsible Party:** **Tamsworth Electrical Constructors Inc. (R. Vance)** must either enclose the `1,500 V dc` string home runs in the trench in a continuous underground raceway coupled to the 2 in. PVC-coated rigid steel stub-ups (`600 mm` cover satisfies **Table 300.50** for raceway), OR direct-bury direct-burial-listed PV wire at **`\u2265 750 mm` (`30 in.`)** cover with **300.50(C)** bushings at the stub-up ends, apply **690.31(B)(1)** DC raceway markings, revise `Drawing E-301 Rev B`, and obtain AHJ approval.\n\n8. **Operational Requirement During Pre-PTO Backfeed (`ISA-Halbray-Field-Site-3`)**\n   - **Requirement:** Throughout the pre-PTO backfeed, **Halbray Community Solar LP** and **Tamsworth Electrical Constructors Inc.** must keep all 12 inverters (`8 x NG-XT-225` and `4 x NG-XT-125` across `SK-1` to `SK-4`) disabled and prohibited from parallel operation while pre-start checks are performed on utility power.\n\n---\n\n### Items Already Accepted or Under Separate Review (Not Blocking Customer Backfeed Resolution)\n- **Utility RTU & POI Recloser:** Accepted by SCADA/EMS Engineering (Marcus Deele, `07/02/2025`, `TCS-02 \u00a73.6`) and Distribution Protection Engineering (N. Rennick)** prior to `07/29/2025`.\n\n2. **Collection Switchboard `CSB-1` Main Circuit Breaker (`2,000 A`) Undersized for 125% of Combined Continuous Inverter Output Current (`2,075.0 A` / `2075.0 A`) (`Drawings E-201 Rev B` & `E-301 Rev B`)**\n   * **Defect / Blocker:** Each skid outputs `2 \u00d7 162.4 A + 90.2 A = 415.0 A` continuous (`1.25 \u00d7 415.0 A = 518.75 A`), giving a combined continuous inverter output current of `4 \u00d7 415.0 A = 1,660.0 A` (`1660.0 A`) across all four skids (`SK-1` to `SK-4`) through `CSB-1`'s main breaker to `T-1`. Under **2023 NEC 690.8(A)(1)(a), 690.9(B), 705.28(A), 705.30(B), 215.2(A)(1)(a), and 215.3**, the overcurrent device must be rated at least `1.25 \u00d7 1,660.0 A = 2,075.0 A` (`2075.0 A`), for which the next standard rating under **NEC 240.6(A)** is **`2,500 A`**. `CSB-1` currently has a **`2,000 A` main breaker** (selected above `T-1`'s `1,804.22 A` [`1,804 A`] secondary full-load current without applying the 125% continuous-current factor to the `1,660.0 A` inverter output; an 80%-rated `2,000 A` breaker is limited to `1,600 A` continuous `< 1,660.0 A`).\n   * **Required Action & Responsible Party:** **Tamsworth Electrical Constructors Inc. (R. Vance)** must either replace the `2,000 A` main breaker in `CSB-1` with a **`2,500 A` main breaker** (`800 V, 65 kA`, matching the `2,500 A` bus and within the 250% secondary limit of **NEC Table 450.3(A)** for `T-1`), OR provide manufacturer listing documentation demonstrating that the `CSB-1` switchboard assembly and its `2,000 A` main breaker are listed for **100% continuous operation** under **NEC 690.9(B) Exception / 705.30(B) Exception / 215.3 Exception No. 1**, revise `Drawing E-201 Rev B`, and obtain AHJ approval.\n\n3. **Missing Multiple-Source Busbar Warning Label on `CSB-1` (and `ACP-1` to `ACP-4`) and Missing Power-Source Directory Plaque (`Drawing E-201 Rev B` \u2014 `CSB-1` & `Labels`)**\n   * **Defect / Blocker:** On `CSB-1` (`2,500 A` bus), `2,000 A (main) + 2,075 A (125% PV) = 4,075 A > 3,000 A (120% of bus)`, so `CSB-1` relies on **2023 NEC 705.12(B)(3)(3)** (`4 \u00d7 600 A + 30 A = 2,430 A \u2264 2,500 A` bus; similarly, each `ACP-1..4` has `2 \u00d7 225 A + 125 A = 575 A` on a `600 A` bus). However, `Drawing E-201 Rev B` (`Labels`) omits the required **NEC 705.12(B)(3)(3)** permanent warning label (*\"WARNING: MULTIPLE POWER SOURCES SUPPLY THIS BUS. DO NOT ADD LOADS OR ALTER BREAKER RATINGS SO THAT THE SUM OF ALL OVERCURRENT DEVICES ON THE BUS EXCEEDS THE AMPACITY OF THE BUSBAR\"*) and omits the **2023 NEC 705.10 / 690.56(B)** permanent plaque/directory at `POI`/`PSW-1` and `CSB-1` denoting all electric power sources and disconnecting means.\n   * **Required Action & Responsible Party:** **Tamsworth Electrical Constructors Inc.** must install the **NEC 705.12(B)(3)(3)** warning labels on `CSB-1` and `ACP-1..4` and the **NEC 705.10** directory plaques at `PSW-1`/`POI` and `CSB-1`, and update `Drawing E-201 Rev B`.\n\n4. **Auxiliary Panelboard `AUX-1` & Tracker Controllers `TC-1 to TC-8`: Outdoor Enameled Steel Strut, 800 V Utilization Branch Circuits, Disconnecting Means, and Feeder Emergence (`Drawings E-201 Rev B` & `E-202 Rev B`)**\n   * **Defect / Blocker:**\n     1. *Outdoor Ferrous Strut Protected Solely by Enamel:* `Drawing E-202 Rev B` (`Mounting`) mounts outdoor `AUX-1` and `TC-1 to TC-8` on *\"1-5/8 in. steel strut, green enamel finish,\"* violating **2023 NEC 300.6(A)** (ferrous metal supports protected solely by enamel prohibited outdoors).\n     2. *800 V Branch Circuits Supplying Utilization Equipment:* `AUX-1` (`800 V, 3-phase, 3-wire, 100 A main lugs`, fed directly at `800 V` from a `30 A` breaker in `CSB-1` without an auxiliary step-down transformer) supplies eight 3-pole `15 A` branch circuits at **`800 V`** (`462 V` to ground) to tracker controllers `TC-1 to TC-8`, exceeding the **600 V between conductors** (and 277 V to ground) branch-circuit voltage limit for utilization equipment under **2023 NEC 210.6(D)(2) and 210.6(C)**.\n     3. *Separate-Structure & Controller Disconnecting Means and HDPE Conduit Emergence:* `AUX-1` is a freestanding main-lug-only panelboard with **eight** branch breakers (exceeding the six-disconnect rule of **2023 NEC 225.33(A)** for a separate structure supplied by a feeder under **225.31** without a local main disconnect); `AUX-1` (beside `TC-1`) is not in sight from distant block controllers `TC-2 to TC-8` across blocks 2\u20138 and lacks specified lockable-open provisions per **2023 NEC 430.102(A) & 110.25**; and the `AUX-1` feeder in `1 in. HDPE duct` lacks a specified rigid metal or Schedule 80 PVC riser stub-up where emerging above grade per **2023 NEC 353.12(1)\u2013(2) & 300.5(D)(1), (4)**.\n   * **Required Action & Responsible Party:** **Tamsworth Electrical Constructors Inc. (R. Vance)** must replace the green-enameled strut with hot-dip galvanized or stainless outdoor strut per **NEC 300.6(A)**; install an auxiliary step-down transformer (e.g., 800 V to 480 V or 240/120 V) and appropriately rated panelboard/controller inputs so utilization branch circuits do not exceed 600 V per **NEC 210.6(D)(2)**; provide a main disconnect at `AUX-1` per **NEC 225.31/225.33(A)** and in-sight or 110.25-lockable disconnects for `TC-1 to TC-8` per **NEC 430.102(A)**; install a rigid metal or Schedule 80 PVC riser stub-up where the `AUX-1` feeder emerges above grade per **NEC 353.12 & 300.5(D)**; and revise `Drawings E-201 Rev B` and `E-202 Rev B`.\n\n5. **Tracker Row Slew-Drive Circuits Wired with Open `Type TC` Tray Cable Along Row Piles Outside a Raceway or Cable Tray (`Drawing E-203 Rev B` \u2014 `Wiring`)**\n   * **Defect / Blocker:** `Drawing E-203 Rev B` (`Wiring`) specifies the 96 slew-drive circuits (`48 V dc, 0.25 kW`, 12 rows/block \u00d7 8 blocks) as *\"3 x 12 AWG Cu Type TC, from each block's controller along the row piles to each row's drive\"*\u2014installed as open cable along the tracker row piles without a raceway or cable tray, violating **2023 NEC 336.12(1)\u2013(4) and 336.10(4), (7)**.\n   * **Required Action & Responsible Party:** **Tamsworth Electrical Constructors Inc. (R. Vance)** must enclose the slew-drive circuits in an NEC-compliant raceway or cable tray system (with sunlight-resistant cable) or replace the open `Type TC` runs with an NEC-permitted outdoor/underground wiring method (or AHJ-accepted protected `Type TC-ER`), revise `Drawing E-203 Rev B`, and obtain AHJ approval.\n\n6. **Skid Inverter Output Circuits Using `Type USE-2` Conductors in Aboveground Rigid Steel Conduit on the Skid Frame (`Drawing E-301 Rev B` \u2014 `Inverter Circuits`)**\n   * **Defect / Blocker:** `Drawing E-301 Rev B` (`Inverter Circuits`) specifies `3 x 1/C 4/0 AWG Cu, 2 kV Type USE-2` (`NG-XT-225` to `225 A` breaker) and `3 x 1/C 1 AWG Cu, 2 kV Type USE-2` (`NG-XT-125` to `125 A` breaker) in *\"Rigid steel conduit on the skid frame\"*\u2014an entirely aboveground installation not emerging from the ground, violating **2023 NEC 338.12(B)(2)**.\n   * **Required Action & Responsible Party:** **Tamsworth Electrical Constructors Inc. (R. Vance)** must either verify/document on `Drawing E-301 Rev B` that the installed conductors carry a dual **`Type RHW-2`** (or `XHHW-2`) rating/marking, OR replace the single-rated `2 kV Type USE-2` conductors in the skid-frame conduits with an NEC-permitted 2 kV conductor type such as **`2 kV Type RHW-2`**.\n\n7. **`1,500 V dc` (Over 1,000 V) PV String Home Runs in Trench at `600 mm` (`24 in.`) Depth / Without Underground Raceway or Stub-Up Bushing (`Drawing E-301 Rev B` \u2014 `Pads`)**\n   * **Defect / Blocker:** `Drawing E-301 Rev B` (`Pads`) routes `10 AWG PV wire, 2 kV` string home runs operating at **`maximum dc voltage 1,500 V`** (over `1,000 V`) from the array into `2 in.
